*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将LLM生成式变异算子集成到搜索式自动程序修复（APR）框架中，应用于Simulink/Stateflow建模的网宇物理系统（CPS）。通过扩展FlowRepair方法，在19个真实故障模型上对比评估后发现，LLM变异算子显著降低了修复性能（plausible patches从18降至4-6），揭示了朴素集成LLM到搜索式APR中的根本局限性，并主张采用结构化变异与生成式引导相结合的混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索式APR依赖精心设计的变异算子，而LLM的进展暗示生成式模型可以替代这些算子来动态提出修复方案。作者希望检验这一假设在CPS的Simulink/Stateflow模型场景下是否成立。

Method: 扩展FlowRepair方法，将其部分变异算子替换为LLM生成的变异，在相同的wall-clock预算下，使用19个真实故障Stateflow模型（覆盖四个CPS领域）进行受控对比实验，评估多种LLM变体。

Result: LLM变异修复在FlowRepair实验设置下显著降低了修复性能：plausible patches仅4-6个模型，valid patches仅4个模型，而原始方法分别为18和16。分析发现LLM在精确符号编辑上存在困难、缺乏行为反馈，且产生的搜索空间噪声大。

Conclusion: 该结果并非表明LLM对APR普遍无效，而是揭示了将LLM朴素集成到搜索式APR中的根本局限，支持采用结构化变异与生成式引导相结合的混合策略。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [181] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了RD-Gen，一个考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成器，用于可复现的实时系统调度评估。该工具支持批量化生成不同参数的随机DAG集，并通过案例研究证明了其能够处理多种问题设置和DAG研究需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统正变得更大更复杂，DAG模型被广泛用于表达任务依赖性和并行性。然而，现有的随机DAG生成工具无法考虑最新的多速率应用需求，研究人员需要自行生成随机DAG集，导致额外的工作量、降低可靠性和可复现性。

Method: 提出RD-Gen，一个考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成器，支持可复现的调度评估，并实现不同参数下随机DAG集的批量生成。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了RD-Gen能够有效管理各种问题设置和DAG研究需求。

Conclusion: RD-Gen填补了现有随机DAG生成工具在支持多速率应用方面的空白，提升了实时系统调度研究的可靠性和可复现性。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的语义块模型来表示软件规格说明，并引入了一个以执行为判据的基准来独立于模型能力评估规格质量。该模型包含语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题，并满足四个机器可检查的良构条件。作者在Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移规格上实例化该模型（18个块、19条依赖边），计算验证表明五层分解将每任务上下文减少约71%，覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类法，且不被替代分区Pareto支配。基准使用固定实现者面板和确定性执行裁判（PostgreSQL 16及真实Oracle实例），通过六项设计研究发现确定性作为形式概念成立，但不足以作为当代LLM实现者的独立经验质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件规格说明的质量评估往往与模型能力纠缠不清，缺乏独立于实现者能力的形式化评估框架。作者希望建立一个既能形式化描述规格结构、又能通过执行结果客观判定规格质量的模型和基准。

Method: 提出语义块形式化模型（包含语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题），定义四个机器可检查的良构条件（无环性、单一所有权、约束支配、完整或歧义停止）。确定性的模型论定义为所有一致实现之间的协议，经验上通过独立实现者的收敛性估算。基准采用固定实现者面板、强制无规格对照臂，以PostgreSQL 16和真实Oracle实例作为确定性执行裁判，并设计六项研究（三项预注册操控和三项诊断分析）。

Result: 五层分解通过依赖闭包将每任务上下文均值减少约71%；覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类法且所有缺口已分流；不被测试的替代分区Pareto支配；从非原始结构定义的引文派生边可在99.9分位恢复。25单元子样本重复运行揭示经验变异性下限，中位臂差分布为14.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 确定性作为形式概念得到支持，但在所评估的当代LLM实现者中，不能作为独立的经验质量指标。语义块模型为规格设计提供了形式化框架，但执行判定基准暴露了LLM实现者的变异性限制了其作为质量度量的有效性。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>


### [183] [Accelerated Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristics for Simulation-Based Scheduling via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19487)
*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用Claude智能体AI对Python项目调度仿真代码进行系统性重构的方法，在HPC环境中将测试运行时间从1298秒降至200秒以下，且输出结果不变，每年节省四百万核心小时（约32万新西兰元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python在科研中广泛使用但定制代码在实验规模扩大时运行缓慢，尤其在离散事件项目调度仿真中，顺序状态更新、嵌套循环和面向对象结构限制了编译型数值库和GPU加速库的优势。传统优化需要迭代式性能剖析、重构、测试和验证，但研究人员往往缺乏时间或底层软件优化专业技能。

Method: 使用Claude智能体AI进行系统性重构：以代表性基准测试和正确性检查为指导，智能体自动识别性能瓶颈、实施针对性优化并评估优化效果，同时研究人员保留最终控制权。该方法在真实项目调度工作负载和高性能计算环境中实施。

Result: 测试运行时间从1,298秒显著缩短至200秒以下，且输出结果完全不变；每年节省四百万核心小时，折合32万新西兰元的经济成本。

Conclusion: 智能体AI可以在缺乏底层优化专业技能的科研场景中有效辅助代码性能优化，通过保留研究人员的最终控制权实现人机协同，在保证结果正确性的前提下大幅提升运行效率并产生可观的经济效益。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [184] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG）的自动化框架，用于生成符合欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）要求的保证案例（ACs），并通过形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑，帮助中小型企业（SMEs）降低网络安全合规评估的资源成本。在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例中，该框架生成了70个ACs，具备高接地密度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟CRA合规认证对中小企业而言是资源密集型挑战，因为网络安全合规性评估过程复杂，但这对证明法规合规性和确保产品安全性至关重要。现有方法缺少自动化、可扩展且可解释的途径来生成认证证据。

Method: 提出一个自动化框架，采用基于形式化Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑的智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG），系统性地映射技术文档需求，从而简化认证证据的生成流程。同时引入一个基于自然语言推理（NLI）的评估器来验证证据的接地性和可追溯性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例研究中，该框架生成了70个ACs，每个AC平均约4.4个工件的高接地密度；NLI评估器达到0.88的准确率；专家验证的可信度评分为3.06，支持可解释的论证证明。

Conclusion: 这项工作为 practitioners 提供了一种可扩展、可解释的自动化方案，用于执行强制性的CRA合规评估，在减少人工工作量的同时保持透明的决策支持，对中小企业尤其有价值。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>


### [185] [Trace-Based Execution-Level Observability of VDM-SL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19510)
*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在VDM形式化规范中记录并利用执行轨迹（包括赋值、操作调用和返回语句）的方法，使操作的内部行为得以持久化并作为基于状态的模型加以分析，并在ViennaTalk中实现了事件数据模型及其可视化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM通过数学定理证明和模拟执行来追求严格验证，但现有的动画和调试器虽然能帮助用户跟踪操作内部行为，却无法将执行轨迹持久化用于后续分析。作者希望通过记录执行轨迹，使内部行为成为可分析的状态模型。

Method: 提出记录和利用执行轨迹的方法，轨迹包含赋值、操作调用和返回语句等事件。设计了执行轨迹中事件的数据模型，并在ViennaTalk中实现该模型，同时应用于可视化。

Result: 在ViennaTalk中成功实现了执行轨迹事件的数据模型，并将其应用于可视化的场景展示。

Conclusion: 通过持久化的执行轨迹，VDM规范的内部行为可以被记录并作为状态模型进行分析和可视化，增强了规范验证和分析能力。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [186] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对三种Java垃圾回收器（Serial、Parallel、G1）在三个应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK、Oracle JDK）下进行了受控实证评估。结果表明Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8 J），但统计上不存在可靠的收集器效应；工作负载强度才是能耗的主要驱动因素，且不同JDK间无显著能耗差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾回收器的选择虽是一项低成本的配置决策，但能显著影响Java应用的性能和能耗。然而，现有的关于收集器间能耗排名的结论在不同应用、工作负载强度和JDK发行版之间是否具有普适性仍不明确，需要系统性实证研究来验证。

Method: 采用受控实证评估方法，在3个Java应用×3种工作负载强度×2个JDK发行版（OpenJDK与Oracle JDK）的组合下，使用EnergiBridge测量处理器封装能耗和执行时间，并推导互补的能效指标。采用RCBD ANOVA进行统计分析，并计算能耗与执行时间的相关性。

Result: Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8 J），Serial次之（857.6 J），G1最高（969.0 J），但RCBD ANOVA未证明存在统计上可靠的收集器效应。工作负载强度是能耗的显著驱动因素，重负载显著高于轻、中负载。能耗与执行时间呈中等正相关（r=0.33），OpenJDK与Oracle JDK之间无显著能耗差异。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍最优的节能垃圾回收器；工作负载强度是管理Java能耗更可靠的抓手。垃圾回收器的选择应被视为应用特定的调优决策，需在目标系统上进行实测，而非依赖聚合排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [187] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了反馈驱动的LLM测试生成中"执行反馈是自我验证信号"这一假设，发现单一程序作为参考oracle会产生虚假的故障检测和进化收益。通过142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务的大规模实验，证明单参考oracle将进化收益虚增9.46-14.85个百分点，并提出了审计-安慰剂协议来分离验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈常被当作LLM生成测试的自我验证信号，但当生成输入在单一被接受程序上执行并将其输出作为ground truth时，无效或欠规范输入会造成虚假的故障检测和表面的进化收益。这一失败模式需要被系统审计。

Method: 使用142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务，两个代码模型、三个种子和故障交叉拟合的真实提交进行审计。采用三程序面板一致作为外部基准进行对比，比较单参考oracle与面板oracle的进化收益差异；引入密度匹配的安慰剂对照三轮反馈循环；由两名软件工程博士生进行盲法语义审计。

Result: 外部输入中三个被接受实现一致的情况下，生成输出仅匹配面板27.79%和50.12%；单参考oracle将进化收益虚增9.46-14.85个百分点；审计后等预算独立重采样优于基于突变的进化6.01-18.83个百分点；真实反馈与安慰剂差异微弱（外部+0.13和-0.50分，保留+1.99和+0.28分）；94.41%的面板否定输入被判为无效，但3.60%被判定为有效。

Conclusion: 单一参考oracle会系统性地高估自进化测试生成器的收益。面板不一致信息有价值但并非语义证明。需要采用审计-安慰剂协议来严格评估自进化测试生成器的真实能力，区分验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈贡献。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [188] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Loreley，一种将Quality-Diversity（QD）存档与顺序智能体搜索相结合的方法。与顺序冠军编辑（丢弃替代分支）和独立根提议（保留广度但重新开始）不同，Loreley在QD存档中保留完整仓库状态，并将它们作为父代或上下文用于后续编辑。通过在Zstandard实验中对比三种策略（7个配对块×48个作业×3种策略，共1008个候选作业），结果显示QD在48个作业时比顺序冠军低0.135%，比独立根高0.320%，但两种对比均未确立QD优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序智能体搜索会从其当前冠军状态累积更改但丢弃替代分支，而独立提议保留广度但每次从根重新开始。作者希望设计一种既能保留搜索广度（替代分支）又能利用已有状态作为后续编辑上下文的方法，从而探索Loreley是否通过其"踏脚石"（stepping-stone）机制带来端点性能优势。

Method: Loreley在Quality-Diversity归档中保留完整仓库状态，将这些状态作为父代进行采样或作为后续编辑的上下文。候选对象是在隔离的worktree中产生的Git提交，由项目提供的评估器评判。实验设计为匹配的Zstandard实验：7个配对块，每种策略每块48个物理候选作业（共1008个），采用根初始化，并保持各策略原生的并发设置。验证在预算检查点选择获胜者，隐藏保留集评估固定候选。

Result: 在48个作业时，QD比顺序冠军低0.135%（95% BCa区间：-0.556%至+0.161%），比独立根高0.320%（区间：-0.082%至+0.686%）。两种对比均未建立QD的统计优势；顺序冠军具有最高的48作业观测均值和中位数。存档保留和后期采样确实发生：7个最终QD获胜者中有4个在主要父系祖先中具有非在位状态（在回顾性单在位规则下），加入灵感边后升至6个。三个早期能力活动在两个Python库和另一个Zstandard修订版中产生了第4代多文件改进。

Conclusion: Loreley确实参与了预期的踏脚石机制（存档保留与后期采样均有发生），但受控实验未显示48作业时的端点收益。QD在终点性能上未优于顺序冠军，其与独立根的对比也未能确立明确优势。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>


### [189] [A Fully Automated, Deployment-Aware Testing Pipeline for IoT-Based Automotive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19752)
*Denesa Zyberaj,Roman Vintonyak,Pascal Hirmer,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的、部署感知的汽车嵌入式软件测试流水线，结合需求驱动的测试/代码生成、LLM/VLM辅助以及人在回路（human-in-the-loop）审校，并通过Eclipse openDuT支持分布式执行。在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例研究中实现了9项需求的完整功能覆盖和100%的Gherkin生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆中的嵌入式软件测试面临系统复杂性高、架构去中心化、安全性和性能约束严格等挑战，需要减少手动工作量并提高测试一致性，同时支持跨组织、跨地域的协同测试。

Method: 提出一个端到端测试流水线：1）需求驱动的测试与代码生成；2）利用LLM和VLM辅助生成测试用例与代码；3）人工在回路中审校以保证质量；4）基于Eclipse openDuT实现灵活的分布式部署，支持跨地理位置的cyber-physical和IoT基础设施，优化节点可用性和跨组织协调。

Result: 以儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）为案例，实现了全部9项需求的功能覆盖率达到100%，在受控需求集上Gherkin生成准确率达到100%。通过Eclipse openDuT在跨地理位置的ECU上进行分布式测试执行，验证了该流水线适用于OEM-供应商测试工作流。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效减少了手动测试工作并提升了测试一致性，LLM/VLM与人工干预结合的方式可实现高准确的测试生成，且分布式部署能力使其适配实际汽车行业OEM与供应商间的协同测试场景。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [190] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PRAXIS框架，通过模拟人类开发流程从代码库中提取、表示和复用隐性知识，帮助LLM代理提升领域特定代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在通用软件工程任务上表现优异，但在领域特定代码生成上表现不佳。根因是代理缺乏开发者通过实践内化但从未文档化的隐性知识，包括领域业务规则、接口契约和操作约定。这些知识深埋于领域代码中，分散在代码实体及其依赖关系之间，难以检索或学习。

Method: PRAXIS框架包含三个步骤：(1) 通过模拟目标代码库中的人类开发工作流来获取隐性知识；(2) 将知识蒸馏为基于代码依赖图组织的结构化单元；(3) 在代码交互时主动将相关知识呈现给代理。

Result: 实验表明PRAXIS优于配备强大代理搜索能力的SOTA代理，以及基于经验和基于技能的方法。该框架能无缝集成到各种代理框架和LLM中，持续带来性能提升，并支持持续演化，性能随实践积累稳步提升。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地提取、表示和复用隐性知识，PRAXIS有效解决了LLM代理在领域代码生成中缺乏隐性知识的问题，且具备良好的泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [191] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对VDM形式化方法中证明义务生成工作的更新，改进了循环不变量、新增循环变体、改进操作调用的证明义务（包括隐式声明的操作和规范语句），并为操作添加了递归度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM形式化方法通过证明义务确保模型内部一致性，但原有证明义务生成方法存在不足，需要改进以更全面地验证模型。

Method: 改进了循环不变量的处理方式，新增循环变体（loop variants），改进了操作调用的证明义务生成（涵盖隐式声明操作和规范语句），并为操作添加了递归度量（recursive measures）。

Result: 通过示例展示了各项新特性，验证了改进后的证明义务生成方法能更全面地处理循环、操作调用和递归等场景。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了VDM证明义务生成的能力，使其能覆盖更多验证场景，增强了VDM模型验证的完整性和可靠性。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [192] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repo0是一个面向零到全代码生成的连续结构演化框架，通过双有向无环图（Dual-DAG）维护显式架构状态，迭代演化组件边界直至结构收敛，再指导测试驱动开发代码生成，在六个真实仓库上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在代码生成上虽有进展，但多数系统假设预定义仓库架构，无法处理从自然语言需求直接构建完整软件项目的零到全代码生成场景，该场景要求在整个开发过程中保持模块化仓库架构。

Method: Repo0维护一个由需求级DAG、组件级DAG及二者对齐关系组成的Dual-DAG显式架构状态。从自然语言需求出发，通过结构动作在模块化度量指导下迭代演化组件边界，直至结构收敛；收敛后的架构再指导测试驱动开发的代码生成。

Result: 在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2评估，Repo0在所有设置下取得最高的功能覆盖率和通过率。相比最强仓库规划基线RPG，功能覆盖率最高提升20.08个百分点，通过率最高提升29.74个百分点。

Conclusion: 消融实验和结构演化分析表明，Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化引导的结构演化以及显式结构收敛对于零到全代码生成至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了使用Cypress框架对开源Web应用进行端到端自动化测试的有效性，通过27个测试用例从执行速度、可靠性和可维护性三个维度进行实验分析，结果表明Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合频繁回归测试，少数涉及复杂交互的测试存在flakiness，同时data-cy属性和POM架构能显著降低维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试在Web开发中被广泛使用，但其实际效果受执行时间、结果稳定性和测试套件可维护性等多重因素影响，需要对这些因素进行系统的实证评估。

Method: 选取一个开源Web应用，使用Cypress框架部署27个端到端测试用例，从执行速度、可靠性（多次运行结果一致性）和可维护性（元素定位策略与POM架构的影响）三个维度进行实验评估。

Result: Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合开发过程中的频繁运行；大多数测试用例多次运行结果一致，仅少数涉及复杂交互功能的测试出现flakiness；data-cy属性能显著减少UI变化时的维护开销。

Conclusion: Cypress框架在端到端自动化测试中表现出良好的有效性，尤其在执行效率和稳定性方面；采用弹性元素定位策略（如data-cy）和POM架构是提升测试套件可维护性的关键实践。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [194] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BreakGuard是一个利用LLM为客户端生成测试套件来检测开源库破坏性变更（BCs）的方法。该方法静态提取调用目标库方法的客户端焦点方法，并为每个方法生成测试。在BUMP数据集上89个真实BCs的评估中，最佳配置检测到30.3%的BCs（27/89），每个检测到的BC平均成本约0.90美元，且对崩溃型BCs的检测比行为型BCs更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库迭代更新时可能引入破坏性变更（BCs），打破与客户端的契约并导致客户端应用崩溃。然而客户端侧的测试套件因库覆盖率有限，往往无法检测到这些BCs。因此需要一种自动化方法来生成测试，以帮助客户端开发者及早发现库升级带来的破坏性变更。

Method: BreakGuard首先静态提取每个调用目标库方法的客户端方法（焦点方法）及其调用点，然后为每个焦点方法使用LLM生成测试。测试的判定标准是：在破坏前版本上通过、在破坏版本上失败的测试即检测到BC。实验使用3个LLM（GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B）和三种上下文级别（最小、方法、类级别）进行配置对比。

Result: 在BUMP数据集的89个真实破坏性变更上评估，最佳配置下BreakGuard检测到30.3%的BCs（27/89），平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。成功检测到的BCs涵盖不同库类别（如JSON库、日志、解析），且LLM生成的测试对崩溃型BCs的检测比对行为型BCs更可靠。

Conclusion: BreakGuard证明了利用LLM生成测试来检测客户端破坏性变更的可行性，在合理的成本下能检测到约三分之一的BCs。然而，该方法对行为型BCs的检测能力有限，这提示了未来需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [195] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对两个公共数据集（SWE-chat 557个代理编码会话和AIDev 33,097个代理PR）的行为分析，揭示自主编码代理与文档交互的四个关键发现：代理主要消费面向代理的工件（指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%）；文档咨询与代码编辑的关系不明确（调整后OR 1.33但未调整提升仅为1.05）；缺少基于文档的验证序列且咨询与即时测试负相关（OR 0.39）；咨询多为自发而非失败驱动（70.2% vs 7.5%）。据此提出代理-文档交互的"双叶循环"描述模型，并质疑"可行动性"和"可验证性"作为代理友好文档的假设缺乏行为支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主编码代理在软件开发中占比日益增加，它们与文档的交互行为却完全未知。现有文档实践基于人类开发者假设，缺乏对代理如何、何时、为何消费文档的经验证据。

Method: 采用行为扎根研究法，分析两个公共数据集：SWE-chat（557个代理会话，94,813个开发事件，3,033次文档交互）和AIDev（33,097个代理PR，690,260个文件级变更记录）。使用相邻转移概率、三事件提升度、阶段调整模型（OR）和聚类置信区间等统计方法。

Result: 四方面发现：1) 代理文档工作以面向代理的工件为主（指令文件+工作笔记占60.5%，传统技术文档仅10.6%）；2) 文档咨询与编辑关联证据不一致（未调整提升1.05 vs 调整后OR 1.33）；3) 无显式文档验证序列，咨询伴随测试减少（调整OR 0.39）；4) 咨询70.2%为自发，文档落后于代码（多提交PR中代码先被触碰的概率高4.7倍）。

Conclusion: 提出"双叶循环"描述模型替代线性文档旅程，并表明"可行动性"和"可验证性"这两个被广泛假设的代理友好文档特性缺乏一致的行为支持。发布完整流水线、编码方案和事件级数据供社区复现和扩展。

Abstract: Technical documentation is written for human developers, but an increasing share of software changes is now authored by autonomous coding agents. Which documents they consult, when, and what follows remain unknown. We conduct a behaviour-grounded study of agent-documentation interaction across two public datasets: 557 agentic coding sessions from SWE-chat, yielding 94,813 development events including 3,033 documentation interactions; and 33,097 agentic pull requests from AIDev, with 690,260 classified file-level change records. Four findings challenge current documentation practice. First, agents' documentation work is dominated by agent-facing artefacts: instruction files and working notes account for 60.5% of all documentation interactions, versus 10.6% for classical technical documentation and 1.3% for API references. Second, the link between consultation and code editing is unresolved: the adjacent transition probability is 0.002 and the unadjusted three-event lift 1.05, whereas a stage-adjusted model places it above unity (OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]); documentation creation is elevated unadjusted (lift 1.67) but its adjusted interval includes unity. Third, no explicit documentation-based validation sequence was observed, and consultation is associated with less immediate testing (lift 0.23, cluster CI 0.08-0.45; adjusted OR 0.39 [0.25, 0.60]). Fourth, consultation is self-initiated (70.2%) far more often than failure-driven (7.5%), and documentation trails code: among multi-commit pull requests changing both, code is touched first 4.7x more often. From these traces we derive a descriptive model of agent-documentation interaction as a two-lobed cycle rather than a linear journey, and show that two widely assumed properties of "agent-friendly" documentation - actionability and verifiability - lack consistent behavioural support. We release our pipeline, coding scheme, and event-level data.

</details>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Fairness-Aware Network Embeddings: Methods, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19381)
*Ella Has,Harshith Kumar Yadav,Gaurav Dixit,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于公平感知网络嵌入方法的综合性综述，提出了一种沿三个维度（嵌入方法、公平干预策略、公平目标标准）分类的体系，并比较了群体公平与个体公平以及敏感属性假设，最后讨论了现有局限和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的网络往往反映结构性不平等（如人口失衡、同质性等社会偏见），而忽视公平性的网络嵌入方法会编码并放大这些偏见。因此需要对现有公平感知网络嵌入方法进行系统梳理和综合比较，以指导后续研究。

Method: 提出三个互补维度的分类体系：（1）底层嵌入方法（谱方法、随机游走、图神经网络、贝叶斯、方法无关）；（2）公平干预策略（预处理、处理中、后处理）；（3）公平目标标准（嵌入级或任务级）。同时从群体公平vs个体公平以及敏感属性假设角度进一步比较各方法。

Result: 提供了对公平感知网络嵌入方法的全面分类和比较，识别了不同方法在嵌入方法、干预策略和公平目标上的异同，并总结了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 该综述为公平感知网络嵌入提供了统一视角，可作为开发公平、可信的网络表示学习方法的参考，并指出了若干有前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Network embedding methods learn low-dimensional representations of graph-structured data to support downstream tasks such as node classification, link prediction, and influence maximization. However, real-world networks often reflect structural inequalities arising from demographic imbalances, homophily, and other societal biases, which fairness-agnostic embedding methods can encode and amplify. To address this issue, numerous fairness-aware network embedding methods have been proposed to mitigate bias while preserving embedding utility. This survey presents a comprehensive overview of fairness-aware network embeddings for complex networks. We propose a taxonomy that categorizes existing methods along three main complementary dimensions: underlying embedding approach (spectral, random walk, graph neural network, Bayesian, and method-agnostic), fairness intervention strategy (pre-processing, in-processing, and post-processing), and fairness objective criterion (embedding- or task-level). We further compare methods with respect to group versus individual fairness and assumptions regarding sensitive attributes. Finally, we discuss current limitations and highlight promising future research directions. This survey provides a unified perspective on fairness-aware network embedding and serves as a reference for developing fair and trustworthy network representation learning methods.

</details>


### [197] [Social.Wiki: A Web Held in Common](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19433)
*Theia Henderson,Carmel Schare,Ana Dodik,Clemens N. Klokmose,Ziv Epstein,David D. Clark,David R. Karger*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: Social.Wiki是一个重新构想Web的系统，将网站视为类似维基百科的协作产物而非私有财产。它支持用户通过AI工具共同创建和编辑交互式社交网站（如微博、消息、约会、游戏、拼车等），并采用"多元治理"（plural governance）模型让用户独立选择决定网站编辑策略的规则，同时通过Graffiti去中心化基础设施存储数据，解耦服务器所有权与网站所有权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多人们依赖的网站其所有者与用户利益不完全一致，这是Web的根本问题。已有的协作可塑软件系统（如Webstrates）仅适用于小规模可信群体，缺乏面向公众参与的设计，需要治理机制和细粒度安全模型来解决冲突和保护个人数据。

Method: Social.Wiki建立在协作可塑软件系统（如Webstrates）的基础上，为公共参与而设计，包含：(1) 治理机制以缓解冲突，采用"多元治理"模型让用户独立选择编辑政策；(2) 细粒度安全模型以在可塑环境中保护个人数据；(3) 使用Graffiti去中心化基础设施存储网站编辑和站内数据，解耦底层服务器所有权与网站所有权。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了系统支持的广泛社会技术结构范围，并在黑客马拉松和真实环境中进行了部署验证。

Conclusion: Social.Wiki通过将网站变为可协作编辑的公共资源，配合多元治理和去中心化存储，为Web提供了一种解决所有权与用户利益不一致问题的新范式。

Abstract: Many of the websites people depend on have owners whose interests are not fully aligned with their users. We address the root of this problem by presenting a reimagining of the web where sites are not owned at all but are instead collaboratively produced like Wikipedia articles. We call the system Social.Wiki because it supports the co-creation of interactive social sites, such as those for microblogging, messaging, dating, gaming, ride sharing, and so on. With off-the-shelf AI tools, people with little or no programming experience can edit these sites to better reflect the needs and preferences of their communities.
  Social.Wiki builds on ideas from collaborative malleable software systems such as Webstrates, but is designed for public participation rather than use only within small, trusted groups. To this end, Social.Wiki includes governance to mitigate conflict. To accommodate diverse governance preferences, our model of "plural governance" lets people independently choose the policies that determine which edits to a site they see. Social.Wiki also implements a granular security model to protect personal data in a malleable environment.
  Complementing the decentralized design and governance of Social.Wiki sites, both site edits and within-site data are stored on Graffiti, a decentralized infrastructure, decoupling the ownership of underlying servers from the ownership of sites.
  We evaluate Social.Wiki through case studies that demonstrate the range of sociotechnical structures it supports, as well as through deployments at a hackathon and in the wild.

</details>


### [198] [From Latent Influence to Language: Diffusion-Oriented Content Generation via Audience-Susceptible Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19809)
*Jiaying Lei,Shengqi Dang,Runqian Bai,Ziqing Qian,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了DOCG-AS框架，一个三阶段的面向社交媒体信息扩散的内容生成方法。它先在内容流形上做隐式特征优化找到最优传播特征向量，再用可学习的解码器将其转换为可解释的受众易感特征（自然语言描述），最后利用多组不同初始化得到的特征来改写用户输入生成最终多模态内容。实验表明该方法在预测扩散影响力上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上多模态内容快速增长，信息扩散对广告主和品牌营销者至关重要。人工定制内容费时费力且依赖启发式方法。现有生成模型虽能自动生成内容，但难以将数值化的扩散影响信号转化为可操作的指导，无法捕捉潜在受众易感性，也无法处理异构的受众兴趣。

Method: 提出DOCG-AS三阶段框架：(1) 在真实内容流形上进行隐式特征优化，发现最优传播特征向量；(2) 使用可学习的解码器将该向量显式解码为用自然语言描述的可解释受众易感特征，为内容生成提供指导；(3) 利用不同优化初始化得到的多组受众易感特征，将用户输入改写为最终多模态内容。

Result: 实验表明DOCG-AS在预测扩散影响力方面一致性地优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: DOCG-AS通过将数值扩散信号转化为可解释的受众易感特征指导，有效弥合了扩散影响信号与内容生成之间的鸿沟，验证了该框架在扩散导向内容生成任务上的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of multimodal user-generated content on social media has made information diffusion a critical factor for advertisers and brand marketers. However, manually tailoring content to resonate with specific audiences is labor-intensive and heuristic-driven. While recent generative models offer promising capabilities for automatic content generation, existing approaches for diffusion-oriented content generation still struggle to effectively translate numeric diffusion influence signals into actionable guidance that captures latent audience susceptibility and accounts for heterogeneous audience interests. To address these challenges, we propose DOCG-AS, a three-stage framework for diffusion-oriented content generation. It first performs implicit feature optimization on the realistic content manifold to discover an optimal propagation feature vector. Then, it explicitly decodes this vector using a learnable decoder into interpretable audience-susceptible features described in natural language, providing guidance for content generation. Finally, it leverages multiple sets of audience-susceptible features obtained from different optimization initializations to rewrite the user's input into the final multimodal content. Experiments demonstrate that DOCG-AS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of predicted diffusion influence.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Online Scheduling for Throughput Maximization of Time-varying Markovian Channels with Unknown Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19398)
*Tasmeen Zaman Ornee,Clement Kam,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了下行无线网络中信道统计未知情况下的调度问题。提出以信道状态信息年龄（AoCSI）作为新的充分统计量，将最近观测的CSI及其AoCSI作为调度决策的充分统计量，从而缩减在线学习的状态空间。作者开发了Online-MGF策略，在有限时域内最大化用户期望总吞吐量，实现了亚线性遗憾。对于ON/OFF信道特例，证明了可索引性并推导了Whittle索引的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖利用信道时间相关统计计算信念状态（belief states）来处理过时CSI的调度问题，但信道统计往往未知，导致信念状态不可数，该方法不可行。需要引入新的充分统计量来降低状态空间复杂度，实现在未知信道统计下的高效调度。

Method: 1) 引入AoCSI概念，证明最新观测CSI及其AoCSI是调度决策的充分统计量，缩减状态空间；2) 将问题建模为Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB)；3) 提出Online-MGF策略，实现在线学习并获得亚线性遗憾；4) 针对ON/OFF信道特例，证明索引性并推导Whittle索引闭式解。

Result: 数值结果表明，Online-MGF策略在极少的回合数内即可收敛到已知信道统计下的MGF和Whittle索引策略性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功将未知信道统计下的无线调度问题转化为可处理的在线学习问题，通过AoCSI这一新颖充分统计量克服了传统信念状态方法不可行的困难，并提供了理论上具有亚线性遗憾保障的高效调度策略。

Abstract: We consider a wireless scheduling problem in downlink wireless networks with unknown channel statistics, where a Base Station (BS) sends data to multiple users. The scheduling performance relies heavily on accurate Channel State Information (CSI), which is often costly to acquire. In this paper, CSI is obtained from ACK/NACK feedback, only after each scheduled transmission. Due to limited wireless channel resources, all users cannot be scheduled for transmission simultaneously. Hence, the most recently observed CSI can be outdated. The traditional approach to solve scheduling problems using outdated CSI is to utilize belief states, which are calculated using the time correlation statistics of channels. However, channel statistics are often unknown; consequently, belief states can be uncountable and this approach becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce a new sufficient statistics for the wireless scheduling problem. Towards this effort, we characterize the CSI staleness by the Age of Channel State Information (AoCSI) and show that the latest observed CSI and its AoCSI is a sufficient statistic of the history to make the scheduling decisions. Accordingly, we are able to reduce the state space for online learning. Our goal is to develop an online scheduling algorithm that maximizes the expected sum throughput of all users over a finite time-horizon while satisfying a channel resource constraint. The formulated problem is a Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB). We develop an online Maximum Gain First (Online-MGF) policy, which achieves sub-linear regret on the number of episodes. For a special case of ON/OFF channels, we are able to prove indexability and derive a closed-form expression of the Whittle index. Numerical results demonstrate that the Online-MGF policy converges to MGF and Whittle index policies with known statistics within a very few episodes.

</details>


### [200] [Empirical Evaluation of Cross-Carrier MCPTT & OTT MCX Interoperability in High-Density Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19554)
*Eman Hammad,Derek Ladd,Sridhar Kowdley,Walt Magnussen,Michael Fox*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过在拥有105,000+观众的大学体育场中部署12部智能手机进行多运营商实测，评估MCPTT服务在共享商业基础设施下的跨运营商互操作性和QoS框架。研究发现语音路径故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制，并识别出传输层抖动超过临界阈值导致抖动缓冲器下溢的非线性网络故障模型。优先管理通道可有效绕过拥塞，为应急规划者提供了关键运营洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模人群事件中，多机构应急响应共享商业基础设施时会产生资源争用，影响MCPTT（宽带任务关键型一键通）服务的关键通信可靠性。现有研究缺乏在真实饱和条件下的实证评估。

Method: 在德克萨斯A&M大学Kyle Field体育场（10.5万+观众）的橄榄球比赛中，部署12部相同智能手机横跨多个物理扇区，设计多运营商实地实验。使用POLQA、数据包投递指标和连接率监测自动化语音通话。

Result: 语音路径故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制；识别出尖锐的非线性网络故障模型——传输层抖动超过临界阈值导致去抖动缓冲器下溢，引发结构性音频退化；优先管理通道可有效绕过此拥塞。

Conclusion: 应急规划者应强制要求网络基础设施、端到端网络切片和专用资源分配，以保持传输层抖动低于临界故障边界，确保关键通信的可靠性。

Abstract: Deploying broadband Mission-Critical Push-To-Talk (MCPTT) services over shared commercial infrastructures introduces resource contention during multi-agency responses in mass-crowd events. This study evaluates cross-carrier interoperability and standard versus prioritized quality of service (QoS) frameworks under real-world saturation constraints. We design an empirical multi-carrier field experiment utilizing twelve identical smartphones deployed across multiple physical sectors inside Texas A&M University's Kyle Field during a football game with 105,000+ attendees. Automated voice calls were monitored using Perceptual Objective Listening Quality Analysis (POLQA), packet delivery metrics, and connection rates. The results reveal that voice path failure is isolated to network infrastructure bottlenecks rather than device hardware limitations. Specifically, we identify a sharp, non-linear network failure model where transport-layer jitter exceeding a critical threshold de-jitter buffer underflows, causing structural audio degradation. Priority-managed channels effectively bypass this congestion. This study helps establish an operational insight for emergency planners to mandate network infrastructure, end-to-end network slicing and dedicated resource provisioning capable of keeping transport-layer jitter below the critical failure boundary.

</details>


### [201] [Vorticity Dissipation Based Routing: A Fluid-Kinetic Framework for Loop-Free Transport in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19630)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流体动力学和Helmholtz-Hodge分解的新型路由框架——涡量耗散路由（VDR），将宏观流量通量正交分解为无旋需求和螺线管涡量分量，通过梯度流优化和Lyapunov稳定性分析实现无环、低延迟且可扩展的超密集无线网络路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超密集无线网络中的离散路由协议受制于信令开销和瞬时路由环路，导致无线电资源效率下降；现有的连续介质标量密度方法缺乏描述拓扑异常的矢量几何结构。

Method: 基于Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观流量通量正交解耦为无旋分量（需求驱动）和螺线管分量（环路诱导），定义网络涡量作为量化拓扑低效性的宏观指标；将路由优化建模为涡度泛函上的梯度流，推导涡量耗散方程作为控制动力学定律，并采用Lyapunov稳定性分析证明全局涡度的单调衰减。

Result: 数值结果验证VDR能抑制实际转发环路、降低端到端延迟、保持稳健的分组投递率，在固定区域致密化下呈现近线性扩展性，同时显式考虑依赖网格的泊松求解开销。

Conclusion: VDR框架为超密集无线网络提供了一种可扩展且数学严谨的路由优化方案，通过涡量耗散机制确保系统收敛到渐近无环均衡态，兼顾了性能、稳定性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Discrete routing protocols in ultra-dense wireless networks are constrained by signaling overhead and transient routing loops that degrade radio-resource efficiency. While continuum modeling provides a scalable alternative, existing scalar density approaches lack the vector geometric structure required to characterize these topological anomalies. This paper introduces a fluid-kinetic framework, vorticity dissipation-based routing (VDR), utilizing the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition. We demonstrate that the macroscopic traffic flux can be orthogonally decoupled into a demand-driven irrotational component and a loop-induced solenoidal component representing routing vorticity. Building on this insight, we define network vorticity as a macroscopic metric to quantify topological inefficiency. Routing optimization is formulated as a gradient flow on an enstrophy functional, yielding a vorticity dissipation equation as the governing dynamic law. Lyapunov stability analysis proves that this mechanism ensures the monotonic decay of global enstrophy toward an asymptotically loop-free equilibrium. Numerical results validate that VDR suppresses realized forwarding loops, reduces end-to-end delay, maintains robust packet delivery, and exhibits near-linear scaling under fixed-area densification while explicitly accounting for the grid-dependent Poisson-solver cost.

</details>


### [202] [Digital Tides: A Fluid-Dynamic Framework for Flux-Aware Infrastructure Provisioning in UAV Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19638)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对高频脉动物流无人机群引发的"数字潮汐"（即动态计算工作负载的宏观平流现象），提出了一种基于流体力学的时空框架。通过求解连续性方程刻画工作负载流的宏观速度场，并进一步提出通量感知的非对称激活策略，利用信息通量矢量作为需求传播的运动学前兆，实现解耦的激活/去激活控制，形成主动防护环来补偿MEC容器冷启动等服务设置延迟。理论分析和数值结果验证了该策略可实现零延迟波前跟踪，并在服务可靠性和能耗之间取得帕累托最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态快照分析和局部密度估计无法捕捉计算工作负载的宏观平流特征，导致反应式资源激活存在固有滞后性，在波前推进处造成关键任务服务丢失。需要一种能够预测需求传播方向的前瞻性资源供给策略。

Method: 1) 建立基于流体力学（连续性方程）的时空框架，显式求解以刻画工作负载流的宏观速度场；2) 提出通量感知的非对称激活策略，利用信息通量矢量作为需求传播的运动学前兆；3) 解耦激活与去激活动力学；4) 推导瞬时服务可用性和周期平均能量效率的闭式表达式；5) 构建QoS惩罚度量以评估严格中断约束下的有效能量效率。

Result: 理论分析确认通量信号具有内在空间相位超前特性；所提策略生成主动防护环以补偿服务设置延迟（包括MEC容器冷启动）；数值结果显示通量驱动策略实现零延迟移动波前跟踪，并在服务可靠性与能耗间取得帕累托最优权衡，优于反应式基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于流体的通量感知非对称激活策略能有效应对高频脉动物流无人机群引发的数字潮汐挑战，通过主动防护环机制消除服务设置延迟影响，实现了动态物流走廊中服务可靠性与能量效率的最佳平衡。

Abstract: The emergence of high-frequency pulsating logistics unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms gives rise to ``Digital Tides'', i.e., complex traffic dynamics that challenge sustainable resource provisioning in mobile computing networks. Conventional infrastructure provisioning strategies, which typically rely on static snapshot-based analysis and localized density estimation, fail to capture the macroscopic advection of computational workloads. As a result, reactive resource activation suffers from inherent hysteresis, yielding nominal efficiency gains at the cost of mission-critical service loss at the advancing wavefront. To address this issue, we develop a fluid-based spatiotemporal framework by explicitly solving the continuity equation to characterize the macroscopic velocity field of the workload flow. Building on this framework, we propose a flux-aware asymmetric activation strategy that leverages the derived information flux vector as a kinematic precursor of demand propagation. Unlike symmetric thresholding, the proposed control logic decouples activation and deactivation dynamics. Theoretical analysis confirms the intrinsic spatial phase-lead of the flux signal and shows that the proposed strategy generates a proactive guard ring to compensate for service setup latency, including delays caused by mobile edge computing container cold-starts. We further derive closed-form expressions for instantaneous service availability and period-average energy efficiency. In addition, we formulate a quality-of-service-penalized metric to evaluate effective energy efficiency under strict outage constraints. Numerical results show that the proposed flux-driven strategy enables zero-latency tracking of the mobile wavefront and achieves a Pareto-optimal trade-off between service reliability and energy consumption, outperforming reactive baselines in dynamic logistics corridors.

</details>


### [203] [RFWM: Physics-Guided World Model for Dynamic Wireless Radiance Field Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19709)
*Zijiu Yang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RFWM，一种物理引导的射频世界模型，将多模态物理条件（如视觉动态和AP配置）映射到时空射频场，通过两阶段训练策略（视觉扩散主干适配+ControlNet物理映射学习）和六项物理引导正则化器，在动态和未见环境中实现射频场的泛化生成，性能较现有最优方法提升约7dB（分布内）和3dB（分布外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频辐射场建模对无线网络优化和感知至关重要，但现有基于学习的方法在动态和未见环境中难以泛化。动态场景中射频场随环境和接入点配置变化，传统方法过度依赖稀疏测量而缺乏物理约束，导致泛化能力不足。

Method: RFWM采用两阶段训练策略：(1)第一阶段将预训练视觉扩散骨干适配到射频轨迹，从少量过去射频输入预测射频序列，并用Friis引导先验提供粗粒度衰减指导；(2)第二阶段从零训练ControlNet学习物理到射频的映射，并微调射频适配骨干，同时用六项物理引导正则化器保证细粒度传播一致性。通过跨高度头部在一次前向传播中联合生成查询接收机高度的射频轨迹，并构建包含115个环境、7715个序列（平均33帧）的动态射频场生成基准。

Result: 在115个环境的基准上，RFWM在分布内设置下较现有最优方法MSE提升约7dB，在分布外设置下提升约3dB，展示了在动态和未见环境中的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: RFWM通过融合物理先验（Friis引导）与扩散模型架构，成功实现了动态和未见环境中射频辐射场的泛化建模，为无线网络优化与感知提供了一种物理-数据混合驱动的有效方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) radiance-field modeling is essential for wireless network optimization and sensing, yet remains challenging in dynamic and unseen environments. Existing learning-based methods synthesize RF fields from sparse measurements, but most struggle to generalize to dynamic and unseen environments. To address this limitation, we propose RFWM, a physics-guided RF world model that maps multimodal physical conditions like visual dynamics and AP configurations to spatiotemporal RF fields. RFWM adopts a two-stage training strategy with physics-guided priors and constraints. In the first stage, RFWM adapts a pretrained visual diffusion backbone to RF trajectories to predict RF sequences from a few past RF inputs, while conditioning the backbone on a Friis-guided prior for coarse attenuation guidance. In the second stage, RFWM learns the physical-to-RF mapping by training a ControlNet from scratch and fine-tuning the RF-adapted backbone, while six physics-guided regularizers enforce fine-grained propagation consistency. Cross-height heads then jointly generate RF trajectories at queried receiver heights in one forward pass. We construct a new benchmark of 7,715 sequences averaging 33 frames across 115 environments for dynamic RF-field generation. Experimental results show that RFWM improves MSE by approximately 7 dB and 3 dB over the state of the art under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [204] [Proper Sea Surface Roughness Enhances the Performance of Near-Shore Maritime Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19730)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Shaoshi Yang,Song Zhao,Jinyang Yu,Weiliang Xie,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机几何的物理驱动分析框架，用于近岸海上无线通信的性能分析。该框架用非齐次泊松点过程建模船只空间分布，并通过经典瑞利准则推导新的反射系数，将路径损耗与显著波高显式关联，从而替代理想光滑海面的简化假设。基于此粗糙度感知信道模型，作者推导了上行覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式，首次在动态海况条件下实现了对聚合干扰的可追踪刻画。分析揭示了依赖海况的可靠性-容量权衡：粗糙度引起的镜面反射衰减可抑制相消干涉零点、提升可靠性导向的覆盖性能，但同时降低高信噪比和平均速率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近岸海上无线通信性能分析模型往往依赖过度简化的传播假设（如假设海面完全光滑），无法捕捉海上信道的完整动态特性（如波浪导致的粗糙度效应），因此难以准确评估真实海况下的通信性能。

Method: 首先将船只空间分布建模为非齐次泊松点过程以反映近港口密度的非均匀性；然后从经典瑞利准则出发推导全新的反射系数，将路径损失与显著波高显式关联；最后将此粗糙度感知信道模型整合进随机几何框架，推导出上行覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式；并通过跨频率消融实验验证波长对粗糙度效应的影响。

Result: 分析揭示了依赖海况的可靠性-容量权衡：粗糙度引起的相干镜面反射衰减可以抑制相消干涉零点，从而提升可靠性导向的覆盖性能，但会降低高信噪比和平均速率性能。跨频率消融实验确认了粗糙度效应的波长依赖性，表明反射系数必须按工作频率进行评估。实测数据支持粗糙度敏感的反射机制，但粗糙海况下的直接VHF验证尚不可得，相应结果被视为基于模型的预测。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在动态海况条件下实现了对聚合干扰的可追踪解析刻画，为近岸海上通信系统的性能分析与优化提供了更贴近实际的理论基础。研究同时指出海况相关的可靠性-容量权衡是多方面性能之间的折衷，且反射系数需按频率评估。

Abstract: Accurate performance analysis for near-shore maritime wireless communication is essential for ensuring robust and reliable operations. However, existing analytical models often rely on oversimplified propagation assumptions, such as a perfectly smooth sea surface, which fail to capture the full dynamics of the maritime channel. In this paper, we develop a physically grounded analytical framework using stochastic geometry that bridges this gap. The spatial distribution of vessels is modeled as a non-homogeneous Poisson point process to reflect realistic near-port densities. We replace the idealized smooth-sea assumption by deriving a novel reflection coefficient from the classical Rayleigh criterion, which explicitly links the path loss to the significant wave height. Integrating this roughness-aware channel model into the stochastic geometry framework, we derive new analytical expressions for the uplink coverage probability and average ergodic rate, providing the first tractable characterization of aggregate interference under such dynamic conditions. The analysis reveals a sea-state-dependent reliability--capacity trade-off: roughness-induced attenuation of the coherent specular reflection can suppress destructive-interference nulls and improve reliability-oriented coverage, while reducing high-SINR and average-rate performance. Available measurements support the underlying roughness-sensitive reflection mechanism, but direct VHF validation under rough sea conditions remains unavailable; the corresponding rough-sea results are therefore interpreted as model-based predictions. A cross-frequency ablation further confirms the wavelength dependence of the roughness effect and shows that the reflection coefficient must be evaluated for the operating frequency.

</details>


### [205] [Using Zone-Disjoint Multi-Path Routing Algorithm for Video Transmission over Ah-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20148)
*Ali Asghar Khavasi,Nastooh Taheri Javan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多路径路由算法，用于在adhoc网络中实现区域不相交（zone-disjoint）路径选择，以改进多路径视频传输性能。该算法考虑CSMA/CA介质访问机制的影响，通过选择区域不相交路径避免节点不相交路径中存在的邻居干扰问题，从而降低端到端延迟和误码率（BER），较已有算法取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在adhoc网络的网格拓扑中，找到多路径看似简单，但由于CSMA/CA效应，路径并非完全不相交。节点不相交路径虽然看似独立，但中间节点为尊重邻居需保持静默，导致端到端延迟增加和BER劣化。因此需要一种考虑区域干扰的路径选择算法。

Method: 提出一种新的多路径路由算法，选择区域不相交（zone-disjoint）路径而非节点不相交路径，并结合适当的视频传输方案，在adhoc网络上进行多路径视频传输。

Result: 通过使用该新算法并结合适当的视频传输方案，在端到端延迟、BER等性能指标上相较于先前使用的算法取得了更优的结果。

Conclusion: 区域不相交多路径路由算法能够有效规避CSMA/CA带来的邻居干扰问题，是多路径视频传输在adhoc网络中的优选方案。

Abstract: Finding multi-path routes in ad hoc networks due to their grid topology seems to be a trivial task, but because of CSMA/CA effects in these networks found paths are not completely disjoint unless an appropriate algorithm have taken into account. If such an algorithm provided and designed carefully it could improve multi-path video transmission over these kinds of networks. By using node-disjoint paths, it is expected that the end-to-end delay and BER in each case should be independent of each other. However, because of natural properties and medium access mechanisms in ad hoc networks the end-to-end delay and also BER between any source and destination depends on the pattern of communication in the neighborhood region. In this case some of the intermediate nodes should be silent to reverence their neighbors and this matter increases the end-to-end delay. To avoid this problem, multi-path routing algorithms can use zone-disjoint paths instead of node-disjoint paths. In this paper we demonstrated a new multi-path routing algorithm that selects zone-disjoint paths that in addition used for video multi-path transmission over ad hoc networks. It is shown that by using this new algorithm along with choosing an appropriate scheme for video transmission over discovered paths, our approach receives enhanced results comparing previously used algorithms.

</details>
